The Law of Market and Neighborhood Watchers No. 772, 1966, lost its current status with the changing legislation, remained far from the pursuit of legal and social conditions and unable to meet the needs. For this purpose, law proposals have been made to determine the duties and status of the market and neighborhood watchmen, employment, working conditions, the current legislation of their rights and the legal status of their rights. With this proposal, special conditions for the market and neighborhood watch candidates are set by regulations to be issued by the Ministry of the Interior on the opening of courses, the opening of courses, the hours and times of training for the guards, and other issues related to the implementation of this law concerning the duties of the guards. As part of the public aid mission of the market and neighborhood watchers, the duty of residents to provide rest, health and safety for residents is considered: to help our citizens who have been accidented, sick or in need of help on the road; to provide information to the nearest law enforcement unit of women, children and people, disabled and helpless; to provide information to citizens who are applying to ask for a neighborhood or street; to meet requests for help in birth, death, disease, accident, fire, and disaster; to prevent or to notify the law enforcement unit of the animal that threatens public health. It is considered to be a preventive, protective and legal duty for the market and neighborhood watchmen as part of public order and public safety. In order to prevent a crime or crime from being committed, the arrest of the perpetrators of crimes or crimes committed after the crime has been committed, the identification of those who have been given a warrant or forced to bring them in; the identification of persons whose lives, body integrity or property or possible danger to the community is organised within the hours of duty, in which they can stop persons and vehicles in the areas of duty, in order to prevent them. There have been statements made in public that would lead to a false perception, as if it were a separate law enforcement agency, in some circles that oppose the law. The market and neighborhood watchmen will have a law enforcement force that will help the general law enforcement force. At the same time, they will have the power to use arms and to use force in the 16th Article of the Law on Police Duty and Law on Rights. In fact, it is possible that the street and the neighborhood guards, who were originally law enforcement officers, would encounter any crime or criminal at the scene of the crime. It's not uncommon for him to use a gun and force at the time of an emergency response. Weapons and force enforcement here are not a arbitrary practice, but the city and the neighborhood guards are required to use it. Guarding extends to the Ottomans in our own history. The Ottomans say that this group of professions, called the chieftainship of the Ottomans, was carried out during the days of Sultan Mehmet the Conqueror. In the modern sense, the watchdog was first set up in 1914 with the Law on the Market and the Mahallat Watchmen, and continued as a deputy law enforcement officer with Law 772, and was last appointed in 1974, but all present guards were removed from the streets and assigned to assist services in 1991. The 8,152 guards who were on duty ahead of the 2007 General Elections were made police with a sudden decision, and thus were removed from the night guard's post, which is based on Ottoman origins and is called the "pazvant." Maybe it was the best decision ever made about the guards. It was announced that the order issued on March 18, 2016 in the Official Gazette was to be taken into custody, and the purchase of the guards began again, starting with the start of five pilots from the province. On 29 January 2020, an offer of the Market and Neighborhood Watch Act, which includes asking the guards for IDs and providing judicial duties, was adopted by the Turkish Grand National Assembly's Interior Commission. When the proposal is examined, all of the powers that have been granted sound good in terms of public safety. Who wouldn't want a criminal caught or to live safely in a dangerous neighborhood? But the goose's foot isn't like that at all. These powers are already available to the guards and the police and the gendarmes.
